# Skills · R · part 181

840 entries, rna-structure-lineplot-visualization to roadmap-docs. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **rna-structure-lineplot-visualization**: Use when you have an RNA secondary structure (CT or dot-bracket format) plus a per-nucleotide Shannon entropy file and need per-nucleotide BPC and smoothed SE line plots (e.g. reproducing Supplementary Fig. S2A/B for HCV_Jc1). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-structure-lineplot-visualization))
- **rna-structure-ncrna-search**: Searches for non-coding RNA homologs and classifies RNA families using Infernal covariance model searches against the Rfam database. Identifies structured RNAs by sequence and secondary structure conservation. Use when querying sequences against Rfam, building custom covariance models for novel RNA families, or classifying non-coding transcripts by family. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/rna-structure-ncrna-search) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **rna-structure-secondary-structure-prediction**: Predicts RNA secondary structures using minimum free energy folding and partition function analysis with ViennaRNA (RNAfold, RNAalifold, RNAcofold). Computes base-pair probabilities, centroid structures, and consensus structures from alignments. Use when predicting RNA folding, evaluating structural stability, or comparing structures across homologs. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/rna-structure-secondary-structure-prediction) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **rna-structure-structure-probing**: Analyzes experimental RNA structure probing data from SHAPE-MaP and DMS-MaPseq experiments using ShapeMapper2. Converts mutation rates to per-nucleotide reactivity profiles that constrain structure prediction. Use when processing SHAPE-MaP or DMS-MaPseq sequencing data to obtain experimental RNA structure information. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/rna-structure-structure-probing) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **rna-transcript-abundance-simulation**: Use when simulating ONT direct-RNA (RNA004) nanopore signals from a transcriptome where per-transcript abundance and truncation profiles must be honored, e.g. reproducing HG002-RNA004 experiments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/rna-transcript-abundance-simulation))
- **rna-transcript-dataset-construction**: Use when building training and test FASTA sets of mRNA, their CDS, and lncRNA transcripts from curated annotations (e.g., NCBI RefSeq or GENCODE) to train or benchmark lncRNA identification models. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-transcript-dataset-construction))
- **rna-velocity**: --&gt; ([mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-/tree/HEAD/Skills/Genomics/Single_Cell/rna-velocity) · ★36 · 2 copies)
- **rna-velocity-agent**: --&gt; ([FreedomIntelligence/OpenClaw-Medical-Skills](https://github.com/FreedomIntelligence/OpenClaw-Medical-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/rna-velocity-agent) · ★3.1k · 13 copies)
- **rna-velocity-agent**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-ml/rna-velocity-agent) · ★745)
- **rna-velocity-agent**: AI-powered RNA velocity analysis for predicting cellular state transitions, differentiation trajectories, and dynamic gene regulation from single-cell RNA sequencing data. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/rna-velocity-agent) · ★104 · 3 copies)
- **rna-velocity-agent**: ---name: rna-velocity-agent ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry-data](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry-data/tree/HEAD/ai-ml/rna-velocity-agent) · ★30 · 2 copies)
- **rna-velocity-alignment-score-computation**: Use when you have an AnnData object with spliced/unspliced layers, scvelo RNA velocity, and an ensemble of DTW-aligned simulated trajectories, and need per-cell scores, pseudotime, and cell fate probabilities. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-velocity-alignment-score-computation))
- **rna-velocity-baseline-comparison**: Use when you need to benchmark an RNA-velocity- or manifold-based cell-cycle method (scVelo dynamical, Cyclum, Cyclone, Revelio, DeepCycle) on mESC or human fibroblast h5ad data and quantify agreement of inferred trajectories with annotated phases or DeepCycle's cell_cycle_theta. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-velocity-baseline-comparison))
- **rna-velocity-benchmark**: Use before designing an RNA velocity tool benchmark, writing a scoring script, or dispatching per-tool sub-agents on CITE-seq or Multiome bone-marrow data (GSE194122 / NeurIPS 2021 reference). ([Thurosse/claude](https://github.com/Thurosse/claude/tree/HEAD/SKILLS/VELOCITY_BENCHMARK/rna-velocity-benchmark))
- **rna-velocity-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when comparing RNA velocity methods on real datasets with known trajectory directions, or when quantitative CBDir/ICCoh benchmark scores are needed to validate velocity estimates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-velocity-benchmark-evaluation))
- **rna-velocity-best-fitted-gene-selection**: Use when a TFvelo run has completed (rc.h5ad output exists) and you need to extract -fitted genes, test KEGG pathway enrichment among them, and verify the sign consistency of top-weighted TFs on specific pathway gene sets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-velocity-best-fitted-gene-selection))
- **rna-velocity-dynamical-model-gene-ranking**: Use when comparing unsupervised feature-selection methods on a differentiation trajectory and you have spliced/unspliced counts so ScVelo's dynamical model can produce a likelihood-ranked gene set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-velocity-dynamical-model-gene-ranking))
- **rna-velocity-estimation**: Use when scRNA-seq data with spliced and unspliced counts (e.g. SMART-Seq2 U2OS cell cycle data) must be converted into velocity vectors and a cell-to-cell transition matrix to support directionality-aware trajectory reconstruction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-velocity-estimation--8ebface7273d0bdb))
- **rna-velocity-estimation**: Use when you have spliced/unspliced count matrices (AnnData/h5ad) from differentiation datasets such as pancreas, dentate gyrus, retina, hindbrain, erythroid or bone marrow, and need directed cell transitions, latent time, or CBDir benchmarking against scVelo. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-velocity-estimation--bf1f63895efaec15))
- **rna-velocity-estimation-spliced-unspliced**: Use when single-cell scRNA-seq data with spliced and unspliced count layers is available and the goal is to visualize predicted transcriptional trajectories as animated arrows/particles in a 3D embedding (e.g., PHATE/UMAP) inside CellexalVR. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-velocity-estimation-spliced-unspliced))
- **rna-velocity-fate-probability-inference**: Use when a pre-processed AnnData with cell-type 'clusters' annotations and a scVelo RNA velocity field is available and you need per-cell fate probabilities or end-fate frequencies toward terminal states (e.g., E15.5 endocrine α, β, ε fates), or to benchmark against CellRank. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-velocity-fate-probability-inference))
- **rna-velocity-inference**: Use when single-cell spliced/unspliced AnnData layers are available and you need velocity fields, latent trajectories, batch-corrected dynamics embeddings, or fate separation across multiple lineages where steady-state RNA velocity assumptions fail. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-velocity-inference))
- **rna-velocity-intron-transcript-extraction**: Use when preparing a kallisto/bustools index for RNA velocity or splicing-aware scRNA-seq quantification, so that pseudoalignment can separate spliced (cDNA) reads from unspliced (intronic) reads. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-velocity-intron-transcript-extraction))
- **rna-velocity-latent-dynamics-modeling**: Use when spliced/unspliced AnnData layers are available and the goal is RNA velocity on multi-lineage, time-dependent, or multi-batch data where strict steady-state assumptions of Velocyto/scVelo fail, or when batch-integrated velocities and latent trajectories are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-velocity-latent-dynamics-modeling))
- **rna-velocity-latent-time-inference**: Use when multi-batch single-cell transcriptomic data captures a continuous biological process (e.g., erythroid differentiation) and cells must be ordered in pseudo-time to validate that learned latent factors show distinct temporal profiles. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-velocity-latent-time-inference))
- **rna-velocity-moments-preprocessing**: Use when preparing an scRNA-seq AnnData (e.g. adata_mESC.h5ad from Zenodo) that lacks imputed 'Ms'/'Mu' layers, as a mandatory step before running DeepCycle or any unspliced-spliced RNA velocity analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-velocity-moments-preprocessing))
- **rna-velocity-pseudotime-estimation**: Use when scRNA-seq data has pre-computed RNA velocity (scvelo/velocyto), root and terminal cell states are identifiable or supplied, and per-lineage pseudotime must be inferred and validated against a known cell type ordering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-velocity-pseudotime-estimation))
- **rna-velocity-report**: Use before compiling the PDF report for an RNA velocity benchmark, so its structure, LaTeX conventions, figures, and tool colour palette match the required template. ([Thurosse/claude](https://github.com/Thurosse/claude/tree/HEAD/SKILLS/VELOCITY_BENCHMARK/rna-velocity-report))
- **rna-velocity-smoothing**: Use when an scRNA-seq AnnData object with spliced counts and scVelo-derived RNA velocity must be denoised into meta-cell-level smoothed velocities as input for trajectory inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-velocity-smoothing))
- **rna-velocity-starter**: Use this starter when a task lands in the `RNA velocity` frontier leaf and the repository has curated resources but no dedicated runtime implementation yet. ([ma-compbio-lab/SkillFoundry](https://github.com/ma-compbio-lab/SkillFoundry/tree/HEAD/skills/transcriptomics/rna-velocity-starter) · ★46)
- **rna-velocity-trajectory**: Infers directional cellular trajectories from scRNA-seq using spliced/unspliced ratios. Use when predicting cell fates, differentiation endpoints, or driver genes from a single snapshot — covers scVelo, VeloVI, Chronocell, and CellRank. Needs intron-aware quantification; for the upstream counting use starsolo-spliced-unspliced. ([tony-zhelonkin/scio](https://github.com/tony-zhelonkin/scio/tree/HEAD/skills/rna-velocity-trajectory) · ★1)
- **rna-velocity-trajectory-inference**: Use when a scRNA-seq AnnData object (with spliced/unspliced counts) needs trajectory inference: recovering parallel branches, within-population sub-flows, and cell pseudotime that direction-free TI methods (e.g., Slingshot) or velocity tools that lack path output (e.g., CellRank) cannot resolve. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-velocity-trajectory-inference))
- **rna-velocity-transition-matrix-construction**: Use when spliced/unspliced AnnData with scvelo-computed RNA velocity is available and a probabilistic cell-state transition matrix is needed to sample differentiation trajectories or estimate root/terminal states. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-velocity-transition-matrix-construction))
- **rna-versus-dna-read-classification**: Use when processing sci-L3 paired-end FASTQ reads from the joint RNA/chromatin-accessibility (RNA/ATAC) co-assay, where reads must be labeled RNA or DNA so downstream splitting can separate transcriptome from accessibility data per single cell. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/rna-versus-dna-read-classification))
- **RNA_Velocity_Agent**: --&gt; ([mdbabumiamssm/AI-Agentic-Skills-by-Dr.-Mia](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/AI-Agentic-Skills-by-Dr.-Mia/tree/HEAD/Skills/Genomics/RNA_Velocity_Agent) · ★7)
- **rnacentral-probing-finder**: Find new RNA chemical-probing (SHAPE / DMS family) datasets in the literature and turn them into validated RNAcentral metadata YAMLs for this repo. Searches Europe PMC and GEO, triages hits (dedup vs repo, open access, own accession), expands accessions into runs, maps samples to the schema, and validates. Use for "find more chemical-probing datasets to add", "scan for new SHAPE/DMS papers", or the periodic dataset-refresh sweep. ([RNAcentral/rnacentral-probing-metadata](https://github.com/RNAcentral/rnacentral-probing-metadata/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/rnacentral-probing-finder) · ★4)
- **rnacentral-search**: Resolve non-coding RNA identifiers, sequences, types, and cross-references through RNAcentral. Use for RNA annotation, accession normalization, ncRNA, or sequence-context research; not as experimental function validation. ([eightmm/codex-science](https://github.com/eightmm/codex-science/tree/HEAD/authored-skills/rnacentral-search) · ★4)
- **rnacentral-skill**: Submit compact RNAcentral API requests for RNA entry browsing, single-entry lookup, and cross-reference retrieval. Use when a user wants concise RNAcentral summaries ([openai/plugins](https://github.com/openai/plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/life-science-research/skills/rnacentral-skill) · ★8k · 32 copies)
- **rnafm**: Skill for RNA sequence analysis with RNA-FM, a foundation model trained on 23 million non-coding RNA sequences. Use this skill when a user wants to embed RNA sequences, predict RNA secondary structure, classify RNA families, or analyze mRNA with mRNA-FM. Also trigger when the user mentions RNA-FM, RNA foundation model, ncRNA embeddings, RNA secondary structure prediction, or RNA language model. ([naity/FM4Life](https://github.com/naity/FM4Life/tree/HEAD/skills/rnafm) · ★2)
- **rnafold-secondary-structure-curation**: Use when candidate dsRNA/editing-enriched regions with genomic coordinates need structural validation or classification (e.g., stem &gt;= 200 bp, &lt;= 20% mismatches, adjusted MFE &lt;= -0.35) before use as training labels or for final dsRNA annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rnafold-secondary-structure-curation))
- **rnaheat**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/analysis/rnaheat) · ★745)
- **rnaheat-vimalinx-bio-studio**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/analysis/rnaheat-vimalinx-bio-studio) · ★745)
- **rnaquast-95-percent-assembled-gene-metric-computation**: Use when benchmarking de novo transcriptome assemblers (e.g., rnaSPAdes, SPAdes, Trinity, RNA-Bloom) on real RNA-Seq datasets and you need a completeness metric comparable across organisms and datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/rnaquast-95-percent-assembled-gene-metric-computation))
- **rnaquast-assembly-quality-evaluation**: Use when you have de novo transcriptome contigs (e.g. rnaSPAdes/Trinity output) plus a reference annotation and need quality metrics such as 95%-assembled genes, isoforms, database coverage, misassemblies, or duplication ratio. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/rnaquast-assembly-quality-evaluation))
- **rnaseh-control-selection**: Use when computing per-region DRIP-seq log2(IP/control) R-loop enrichment over HOT regions or occupancy-binned control regions and a given cell line has no mock control, so the RNaseH-treated DRIP-seq library must serve as control. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/rnaseh-control-selection))
- **rnaseq**: General-purpose RNA-Seq analysis skill. ([HelmholtzAI-Consultants-Munich/rnaseqplugin](https://github.com/HelmholtzAI-Consultants-Munich/rnaseqplugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/rnaseqplugin/skills/rnaseq))
- **rnaseq-alignment-grch38**: Use when RNA-seq reads (e.g. CLL samples like GSE66228) must be converted into an hg38-aligned, coordinate-sorted, indexed BAM suitable for CRIS or comparable BCR-reconstruction tools. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rnaseq-alignment-grch38))
- **rnaseq-analysis**: Analyze bulk RNA-seq count data using DESeq2. Use when asked to analyze RNA-seq data, perform differential expression analysis from count matrices, or when given a GEO accession (GSE*) with RNA-seq data. Handles raw count matrices with QC, normalization, statistical modeling, and gene ID mapping. ([gexijin/vitiligo](https://github.com/gexijin/vitiligo/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/rnaseq-analysis) · ★1)
- **rnaseq-bioc**: Domain reference for working with RNA-seq Bioconductor objects in this app — DESeqDataSet/SummarizedExperiment/SingleCellExperiment structure, assays, normalization math (CPM/TPM/FPKM) and size factors on endogenous genes, feature_class/feature_length conventions, scater QC metrics, filterByExpr, and the DESeq2 dual-LFC (standard + shrunken) result schema. Use when writing or reviewing any code that touches the dds object, its assays, colData/rowData, normalization, QC, or DESeq2 results. ([siwatr/rnaseq_dashboard](https://github.com/siwatr/rnaseq_dashboard/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/rnaseq-bioc))
- **rnaseq-count-matrix-analysis**: Use when you have a count matrix (genes × samples) from HTSeq, featureCounts, or transcript abundance quantification (Salmon, kallisto), a sample metadata table with experimental design, and you need to test for differential expression while controlling false discovery rate via independent. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/rnaseq-count-matrix-analysis))
- **rnaseq-de**: Differential expression analysis for bulk RNA-seq and pseudo-bulk count matrices with QC, PCA, and contrast testing. ([ClawBio/ClawBio](https://github.com/ClawBio/ClawBio/tree/HEAD/skills/rnaseq-de) · ★1.2k · 12 copies)
- **rnaseq-de**: GARS-authored differential expression (PyDESeq2) — the ClawBio rnaseq-de replacement (decision 0029), numerically validated against the leukemia-tall baseline; owns adaptation, DE, figures and report. ([javrodriguez/genomics-agentic-research-system](https://github.com/javrodriguez/genomics-agentic-research-system/tree/HEAD/gars/_system/wrappers/rnaseq-de) · ★1)
- **rnaseq-de-novo-transcriptome-assembly**: Use when starting from FASTQ RNA-Seq reads (single-organism Illumina data, e.g. NCBI SRA accessions like SRR5133163) and a de novo transcriptome assembly with metrics such as 95%-assembled genes/isoforms is required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/rnaseq-de-novo-transcriptome-assembly))
- **rnaseq-deseq2**: ToolUniverse workflow — Rnaseq Deseq2 ([lamm-mit/scienceclaw](https://github.com/lamm-mit/scienceclaw/tree/HEAD/skills/rnaseq-deseq2) · ★251)
- **rnaseq-deseq2**: Production-ready RNA-seq differential expression analysis using PyDESeq2. Performs DESeq2 normalization, dispersion estimation, Wald testing, LFC shrinkage, and result filtering. Handles multi-factor designs, multiple contrasts, batch effects, and integrates with gene enrichment (gseapy) and MedToolkit annotation tools (UniProt, Ensembl, OpenTargets). Supports CSV/TSV/H5AD input formats and any organism. Use when analyzing RNA-seq count matrices, identifying DEGs, performing differential expression with statistical rigor, or answering questions about gene expression changes. ([rbr7/MedClawMini](https://github.com/rbr7/MedClawMini/tree/HEAD/skills/rnaseq-deseq2))
- **rnaseq-deseq2-count-modeling**: Use when computing gene-level differential expression between exactly two user-defined cohorts from a parsed pdacR dataset (GUI list format with $ex, $sampInfo, $featInfo with SYMBOL, $metadata), where the platform type determines the statistical backend. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rnaseq-deseq2-count-modeling))
- **rnaseq-design-matrix-construction**: Use when an RNA-seq count matrix covers more than two conditions, multiple factors, batch effects, or a time course, and the default two-group DEG schema cannot express the contrasts of interest. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/rnaseq-design-matrix-construction))
- **rnaseq-differential-expression**: Perform RNA-seq differential expression analysis between two sample groups from a count matrix. Supports DESeq2-style (via pydeseq2), Welch's t-test, and Mann-Whitney U methods. Outputs full DE results, significant gene table, volcano plot, MA plot, and top-gene heatmap. Outputs feed directly into go-analysis-for-gene-list and gsea-for-ranked-gene-list. ([MDhewei/bioinfor-claw](https://github.com/MDhewei/bioinfor-claw/tree/HEAD/multiomics-data-analysis/rnaseq-differential-expression) · ★65)
- **rnaseq-differential-expression**: `rnaseq-differential-expression` · implementation: `bioagent.skills.omics.rnaseq:rnaseq_differential_expression` · **candidate, not in the stable lockfile** ([psknlr/TCMScience](https://github.com/psknlr/TCMScience/tree/HEAD/BioScience-Harness/skills/candidates/omics/rnaseq-differential-expression) · ★1)
- **rnaseq-dropout-characterization**: Use when a cells-by-variants VAF matrix from scRNA-seq variant calling contains NA/missing entries and you need to quantify how much missingness is attributable to type I drop-out before lineage reconstruction or quality assessment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rnaseq-dropout-characterization))
- **rnaseq-expression-matrix-sample-feature-counting**: Use when reproducing or auditing a published multi-dataset compendium table (dataset, type, sample description, n samples, n features, Used flag) from saved .rds expression objects rather than from publication text alone. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rnaseq-expression-matrix-sample-feature-counting))
- **rnaseq-expression-median-cutoff-filtering**: Use when starting from a raw RNA-seq expression matrix (e.g. &gt;16,000 genes from ArrayExpress E-MTAB-11611) and low-abundance genes must be dropped by a median-expression cut-off before fitting models such as nestcv.glmnet. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rnaseq-expression-median-cutoff-filtering))
- **rnaseq-expression-profiling-integration**: Use when you have processed RNA-seq expression values for genes (e.g. from STAR/RSEM/DESeq2) and want them carried through a CCIVR cis-NAT identification run into Table.csv and Summary.csv outputs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rnaseq-expression-profiling-integration))
- **rnaseq-fusion-candidate-feature-extraction**: Use when preparing spanning-read/split-read candidate fusion evidence (PCAWG positives, artificial chimeric negatives) as features for training or applying the HVLD filter in Anchored-fusion. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rnaseq-fusion-candidate-feature-extraction))
- **rnaseq-gene-annotation**: Use when patient RNA-seq data is keyed by Ensembl gene IDs and must be converted to gene symbols that match nodes of a fixed network model, with unmatched symbols resolved via GeneCards/HGNC aliases.
